# Strategy Findings & Risk Register

Companion to `vsa_strategy_prompt.md`. Captures known weaknesses, improvement ideas, and open questions so they survive across sessions and iterations. Update this document as items are resolved or new risks surface.

---

## 1. Critical risk: look-ahead leakage (ADDRESSED IN v1 SPEC — remain vigilant)

**History:** prior backtesting projects built with AI assistance produced strategies that only appeared to work because future information bled into decisions. This is the project's #1 failure mode.

**Defenses now in the spec** (Non-Negotiable section of the prompt): clock-gated data access layer; explicit bar timestamp convention; truncation-equivalence testing of precomputed features; automated future-perturbation test over random cutoffs; explicit checklist of subtle leaks (full-sample normalization bins/percentiles, unconfirmed swings, HTF state before close, levels defined with later data); leakage tripwire — exceptional results (PF > 2.5, win rate > 65% at ≥ 2R, near-monotonic equity) are presumed leakage until the perturbation test clears that exact configuration.

**Residual risk:** leakage via the *iteration process itself* (see finding 3) and via any manual data handling outside the engine. Never hand-inspect lockbox data.

## 2. Volume data quality (OPEN — first-iteration diagnostic)

Everything rests on Finsa tick volume being a faithful proxy for real participation. This is assumed, not tested.

**Action:** add a volume-quality study to the first iteration's diagnostics: (a) does tick volume show the expected intraday shape (open/close surges, lunch lull, 14:30 London jump on FTSE/DAX)? (b) does it spike on known macro release timestamps? (c) if a sample of real FTSE futures volume can be exported from any broker platform, correlate the two over the same period — this quantifies how degraded the absorption signal is.

**Status:** open. Depends on step-zero verification confirming a volume field exists at all (hard gate).

## 3. Iteration = quiet overfitting (ADDRESSED IN v1 SPEC)

Each review-refine-rerun cycle fits the rules a little more to the same history. Data snooping by another name.

**Defense adopted:** lockbox — most recent ~20% of 1M history (min 4 trading weeks once available), excluded programmatically at the store/loader level, evaluated exactly once when iteration is declared complete. New data accrues to the lockbox between formal iteration rounds.

**Discipline required from us:** the lockbox verdict is final for that iteration round. If it fails, the strategy failed — the answer is not to iterate against the lockbox.

## 4. Thin statistical power early on (ADDRESSED IN v1 SPEC)

Intraday-only + one instrument + gated hypotheses + short accumulated 1M window = possibly ~30 trades in the first backtests, which proves nothing either way.

**Defense adopted:** event-study layer — forward mid returns at +5/+10/+20 Signal-TF bars after every confirmed hypothesis, independent of gating/sizing/EOD/trade simulation, vs. matched baseline bars. ~10× the observations; answers "do the signatures predict anything?" while trade samples mature.

**Interpretation rule:** event-study significance without trade-level profitability means the edge exists but is being eaten by costs/EOD/spread — a fixable problem. Trade-level profit without event-study significance is suspicious (luck or leakage).

## 5. Cliff-edge thresholds (DEFERRED — v2)

Labels are boolean (volume 1.49× baseline isn't "high", 1.51× is), but conviction is continuous. Binary labels waste information and make results threshold-sensitive.

**v1 mitigation:** ±30% sensitivity sweeps already in spec — edge must degrade gracefully.
**v2 candidate:** continuous signature scores (e.g., logistic transforms of rel_volume/rel_spread/close_pos) feeding hypothesis strength directly, replacing label counting.

## 6. Spread vs. stop distance on low TFs (OPEN — first-iteration diagnostic)

On 1M/3M executions, CFD spread may be a large fraction of a tight stop — an apparent 0.3R edge can vanish entirely into the spread.

**Action:** diagnostic reporting average spread as % of stop distance, per hypothesis and per session phase. If it exceeds ~10–15% for a hypothesis, that hypothesis needs wider stops or a higher execution TF.

## 7. Scheduled news contamination (PARTIAL — tag in v1, filter in v2)

A 13:30 London volume spike on CPI day is calendar, not psychology — it will generate false climax/absorption signatures. Session-time normalization cannot catch it (date-specific, not time-of-day-specific).

**v1:** tag all trades and hypothesis spawns within ±15 min of major scheduled releases (manually maintained list is fine initially) so contamination is visible in diagnostics.
**v2:** economic calendar integration as a spawn filter.

## 8. Other v2 candidates (parked)

- Volume profile / higher-quality supply-demand zones as the Layer-1 level source (upgrade path explicitly anticipated in spec).
- Overnight holding with gap-through-stop modeling.
- Trading divergence-migration across timeframes (log-only in v1).
- Reverse-and-flip on opposing confirmations.
- Live narrative mode: same engine fed by scheduled `collect.py` syncs, emitting per-TF phase, open hypotheses with scores, and alignment each minute (Part 10 / path-to-live discussion).
- Futures data with true volume, if Finsa instrument IDs for futures exist.

## Open questions (blocking or near-blocking)

1. **Does the Finsa feed / clean store contain a volume field, and what does it represent?** Hard gate — no engine work until answered. (Step zero.)
2. **How much 1M history is already accumulated per instrument?** Defines the initial backtest window and lockbox feasibility. (`collect.py status`.)
3. What interval is `quarter`, what timezone are timestamps, is there any pagination beyond `l`, and what is the true `l` limit? (Step zero.)
4. Do futures instrument IDs exist on Finsa? (Owner to ask provider.)
5. Do the existing scripts have any bugs affecting already-collected data (timezone drift, silent gaps)? If so, does historical data need re-validation? (Step-zero audit; report before fixing.)

## Decision log (headline design rulings)

- Intraday only; force-flat before cash close; entry embargo 30 min before cutoff. Overnight = v2.
- Intrabar stop/target ambiguity resolved by replaying constituent 1M bars; stop-first if a single 1M bar touches both.
- Execution TF fixed to 1M/3M; embargo overrides refinement.
- Gating is per hypothesis class; H3 range-boundary breakouts permitted in RANGING (`H3_RANGE_BREAK`); H5 exempt with ATR-extension gate.
- Ablation study (location / gating / confirmation independently disabled) mandatory, run on frozen config.
- Portability protocol: tune on FTSE, run frozen config on DAX and NASDAQ; per-instrument re-tuning reported separately.
- Engine reads only from the clean store (`store.py build`); scripts' output paths refactored to project root with equivalence proof; audit before refactor; report data-affecting bugs before fixing.

## Pseudocode review rulings (v1 restatement — logged so nothing lives only in chat)

**Rulings on Claude Code's [A1]–[A17]:** signature ownership rule (climax→H1, UPTHRUST/SPRING→H2, no label spawns two specs — resolves [A1] and its long-side twin); `CONFIRMED_PENDING_GATE` state replaces re-confirmation ([A2]); `EFFORTLESS_DECLINE`/`VALIDATED_DECLINE` added ([A3]); TEST additionally requires volume < 0.5× signature bar rel_volume ([A4]); concurrency rules accepted + blocked-spawn logging ([A5]); [A6]–[A17] accepted as proposed, with [A10]'s near-no-op base/threshold and confirm-bar-delta-before-check documented as deliberate, and [A15]'s pullback volume slope logged for later evaluation.

**Second pass:** structural-core vs. context-qualifier label rule (confirmations reference structural core only — fixes unreachable NO_DEMAND confirmations in H5/H2); refinement invalidation on parent refutation (`REFINEMENT_CANCELLED_REFUTED`), single pending refinement; H3 direction rule for RANGING Signal TF (out of range from the absorption boundary); H1 spawn-level definition under new-low spawns; H4/H3 stops computed at graduation; inclusive lowest-low lookback.

**Forensic pass:** **CRITICAL spec bug — reversal gate lacked a phase-agreement branch**, blocking the framework's canonical trade (upthrust in Context MARKDOWN; climax ending a pullback in Context MARKUP). Fixed: agreement branch first, tagged `REV_WITH_TREND` for separate reporting. Also: descending-TF processing order at simultaneous closes (determinism + perturbation-test stability, unit-tested); H3 zone grows until confirmation, breakout beyond max(level, zone edge); H4 refutation = expansion as pullback state + structural break as trigger; minor mirror/tolerance/lookback fixes.

**New requirement:** synthetic-scenario verification before any backtest (named cases including `REV_WITH_TREND` and `CONFIRMED_PENDING_GATE` paths), reviewed against the psychology appendix.

**Process lesson:** each review layer (document → instantiation → runtime trace) found bugs invisible to the previous one; the critical gating bug survived four document reviews and was only exposed by tracing runtime state. Next bug class lives in execution — hence the synthetic scenarios.

## RULES.md v2 review rulings (2026-08-11)

**All six reviewer findings verified and ratified:** R1 CPG pending window (`pending_gate_max_bars`, default 2× Context/Signal ratio, replaces confirm-window expiry once confirmed — prompt patched); R2 CPG skips strength machinery entirely, floor kill never applies in CPG (prompt patched); R3 two-phase step (evaluate all → resolve conflicts by strength → act once); R4 in-position guard on all entry paths + direct-entry restatement when refinement disabled; R5 evidence semantics — general definition added to prompt, per-hypothesis enumeration required in RULES.md v3; R6 H5 extension gate signed in the fade direction (spec bug, prompt patched). All implementation traps accepted: H3 age re-anchors on each qualifying cluster bar; "outermost in trade direction" wording; H4 pullback boundaries pinned (start = REACTION transition, mean excludes evaluation bar, low at graduation); `zone_level` = the key level Lv; refinement window bar 1 = first exec bar closing strictly after graduation; triggered entry whose fill would land inside the embargo → abandon.

**The three referred rulings:** (1) opposite-direction graduation during pending refinement → **cancel the pending entry** (`REFINEMENT_CANCELLED_OPPOSED`); the opposing hypothesis may begin its own refinement — no position was open, so this is not reverse-and-flip; oscillation bounded by expiry, logged for diagnostics. (2) Confirm-while-understrength → **log-and-lose** for v1 with a `CONFIRM_UNDERSTRENGTH` counter in diagnostics; revisit if the counter is material once thresholds are tuned; no third pending state in v1. (3) `pending_gate_max_bars` default **2× Context/Signal ratio** ratified.

**Spec-bug tally:** two critical defects have now originated in the spec itself (unsigned H5 gate; missing reversal phase-agreement branch) — the spec is not privileged over the restatement; both documents are subject to the same adversarial process.

## Step-zero data findings (from DATA.md — headline implications)

- **Volume is real futures contract counts** (`uk100fut`, ID 70152), not tick volume — the hard gate cleared in the best possible way; absorption hypotheses run undegraded. Cash CFD volume = same series ×4: use ONE series (futures) as canonical; never mix.
- **URGENT operational requirement: rolling ~30-day provider retention.** Data older than the window is unrecoverable. Scheduled sync (daily; hard ceiling every 3 weeks) is now infrastructure-critical — every missed window is permanently lost backtest data.
- `uk100fut` is a RAW SPLICE at quarterly rolls — roll-gap handling (prompt Part 6) applies; signature exclusion around roll transitions; Sep-26 expiry watch item.
- DST session-label bug on US/gold instruments: must be fixed before the NASDAQ portability stage; does not affect FTSE development.

## Backtest campaign log

**2026-08-12 — Backtest v1 (first real-data run; report: `reports/backtest_v1.md`).**
Outcome: **sample starvation** — 16 working-set sessions (exactly 5 consumed by warmup), 2 spawns / 1 confirmation / 0 gated trades on uk100fut (uk100 secondary consistent: 6/2/0). No tuning performed; lockbox untouched. **Compromise in force: `baseline_sessions` 8 / `min_baseline_obs` 5 vs spec defaults 20/20** — all results conditional on this until data allows spec values. The single gate-veto observation (ablated no-gating trade lost 1.04R with negative forward returns at all horizons) is **explicitly non-evidential (n=1)** and is recorded only because the ablation table requires it. New standing diagnostics: per-TF label denominators with conservation-checked spawn fates; label-level event study (primary powered readout for the accumulation window); spread-vs-stop (first reading ≈31% of stop on the one ablated trade — above the 10–15% alarm line in finding 6, thin-sample flagged). Cost-model gap found and fixed during amendment: slippage now applies to entry fills, not just exits. DST session fix (finding: US/gold in_cash now computed in America/New_York) shipped; macro-release calendar scaffolded (`data/macro_releases.csv`, empty — owner to populate). Next checkpoint: first powered label-level readout or walk-forward feasibility, whichever first.

## Standing rulings (2026-08-12 sign-off)

1. **EFFORTLESS_DECLINE excess signal — tracked, not acted.** The −19 bps drift-adjusted wrong-way reading at n=16 is a tracked observation only. If it survives to powered n, it enters the system **only through walk-forward as a candidate hypothesis** — no mid-accumulation rule or threshold changes on its account. (Ruled 2026-08-12; guards the quiet-overfitting defense in finding 3.)

2. **OPEN QUESTION — refinement stop-tightening vs spread burden.** Does execution refinement's R-geometry gain pay for its proportionally larger spread cost? Current evidence (corrected 2026-08-12 — the initially quoted 31% was a hand-derivation error; programmatic figures from logged fill-to-stop): spread ≈ 2.6% of the signature-stop distance (~50 pts) vs ≈ 10.8% of the exec-local tightened stop (12.0 pts) — a ~4× spread-burden ratio, both below the 15% alarm line, single observation each. Weekly campaign reports carry the graduation-stop distribution **split by stop basis** (signature at every graduation, all variants; exec-local at every refined entry) so both populations accrue; resolved by the spec-mandated with/without-refinement comparison once trade samples are powered.

3. **Reports are generated, not written** (effective 2026-08-12, extracted from the 31%-figure correction). Every quantitative claim in any report must be programmatically derived from logged artifacts; hand arithmetic is banned. Implemented: `backtest/report.py` renders `reports/backtest_v1.md` entirely from `reports/backtest_v1/*.json` (trade records now persisted per variant for this purpose); the campaign regenerates the report on every run; the file carries a GENERATED banner. Interpretive prose may carry no hand-computed numbers.


4. **Macro calendar scope contract** (2026-08-12). `data/macro_releases.csv`: timestamps/currency only, red-impact only by construction; used for ex-post tagging (±15 min) and the macro-spike volume check exclusively; **never engine-facing without clock-gating** (actuals are post-release information). Validation-on-load is mandatory for every consumer (`engine/macro.py`: schema, Z-suffix UTC, monotonic, NFP 12:30/13:30 anchor — hard fail). Forward population: `scripts/macro_fetch.py` (FairEconomy JSON feed; HTML scraping banned) writes `data/macro_releases_staging.csv` only; the human merge gate into the live file is deliberate and stays. Deferral: **surprise-conditioned release analysis (actual vs. forecast) — v2 candidate, backfillable from official archives, no capture urgency.**

5. **narrate is diagnostic/non-evidential** (2026-08-13). `engine/narrate.py` (replay + live) is the human front door to the narrative engine: labels/phases/hypotheses/dump only, always narrative-only past the lockbox boundary (scoped loader access, logged on every use; refused without the explicit flag). **No thresholds, rulings, or tracked signals may be touched off any narrate output.** Live mode is v2 machinery arriving early: polling watcher on the collector's rate discipline, always narrative-only, and must not displace the weekly cadence. First replay exercised 2026-08-13 05:00-09:40Z uk100fut (reports/narrate/). Note: that replay printed an EFFORTLESS_DECLINE at the session low preceding the rally - consistent with the tracked signal (ruling 1), explicitly non-evidential, n=1.

8. **Late-session swing confirmability** (2026-08-13, spec gap; ruling to follow). Code today: swing confirmation carries ACROSS the session boundary — the 2k+1 fractal window is a rolling deque that never resets, so a swing in the final k bars confirms only when the NEXT session's opening bars arrive, with fractal neighbors spanning the overnight gap. Worked example: 2026-08-13 14:45-15:15Z range-break lows (10801.8->10785.8) — the only range break of the day — sit inside the final k bars and were unconfirmable at the close; RANGING verdict stood on the confirmed sequence (11:00Z higher high included).

9. **Day-relative vs time-of-day-relative signature detection** (2026-08-13, named question; no changes now). The 06:45Z rejection bar: 98th-percentile spread vs trailing-50 bars, 15th vs its session-time bin — the two normalizations disagree by construction in quiet segments. Adjudicated by the segment-split event study; feeds no thresholds before walk-forward.

10. **Execution-TF classification gap** (open). v1 runs the classifier/context pipeline on Context+Signal TFs only; 1min feeds refinement/broker but is never classified, so narrate --show-tf 1M shows no labels. Spec Part 5 says identical pipeline per TF — known v1 deviation, prioritization pending.

**Rulings closing items 8 and 10 (2026-08-13):** Item 8 RATIFIED as spec — cross-boundary k-window confirmation stands (formalized in prompt Part 3); swings tagged confirmed_across_gap with a weekly diagnostic count; **EOD-provisional confirmation registered as the named v2 alternative** if tagged swings prove pathological. Item 10 CLOSED — classification extended to the 1min TF as OBSERVATIONAL instrumentation: separate drift-adjusted segment-split 1min label event study (uk100fut_exec_label_study.json, autocorrelation caveat embedded, never pooled with 15min), narrate --show-tf 1M live, no threshold or rule consumes 1min labels before walk-forward, formal checkpoint remains Signal-TF. Compute impact measured: full campaign 29s wall (was ~20s), primary narrative artifact 3-4MB — immaterial to the sync-to-report cadence; no sampling scheme needed at current scale.

17. **Observer Cockpit — read-only web UI over existing artifacts** (registered 2026-08-15; build slot AFTER the measured-contract fix + reconciliation prerequisite; may interleave with velocity/efficiency — touches no evidential path). **Stack (operator-specified):** Preact + TypeScript, Vite, yarn; thin read-only Python backend (FastAPI, existing venv) serving session JSONL tail, paper ledger, heartbeat/watchdog stream, lockbox.json zones, clean-store bars — GET/stream only. **Structural fence, two places: the backend exposes no POST/PUT surface at all, and the frontend has nothing to call.** **Design rules, FROZEN:** (1) strictly read-only end to end — no engine, no signals, no writes; killable any moment without system effect; the witness must never notice the observer. (2) Death-minute bound RESOLVED — fossil extracted before the sync could heal it (the race flagged in this entry was WON): store gap 15:05->15:33 open-stamps; last persisted bar opens 15:05 (close 15:06) -> predecessor death ~=15:06-15:08Z, confirming the ~=15:06 estimate to the minute; first post-relaunch bar 15:33 matches the 10-bar catch-up reach exactly. Data hole 27 min (straddles the 15:30Z cash close, consequence-free; reconcile clean); decision hole ~36 min as registered. Evening sync heals the hole; this register is then its sole witness — as intended. **From 2026-08-18, checkpoint.json retires forensic death-dating.**

22. **Session-JSONL persistence — PROMOTED to the next build slot (2026-08-17).** Today demonstrated the gap three ways: the live narrative stream died twice with its file descriptors; the heartbeat stream survived only as an accidental witness; and "where is the continuous narrative stored?" had no good answer. **Spec:** both live loops persist their full event stream (labels, phases, hypothesis events, heartbeats/stderr equivalent) to dated JSONL under logs/, append-per-event, alongside the existing console output. **Scope (reviewer recommendation, operator may override): ALL segments** — paper's perception already runs 24/7; persist what the engine actually sees, with existing observational tagging for non-cash segments, so the persisted narrative matches the continuous view rather than the cash-only console projection. **Test: a killed process's JSONL ends within one event of its checkpoint.** Narrative-only by construction — persists what narrate already emits; feeds no thresholds, rulings, or tracked signals.

**11:46Z cascade analysis (2026-08-17; archived logs/narrate/cascade_analysis_1146_2026-08-17.md; non-evidential).** (1) Ladder rungs VERIFIED as full-perception TFs (complete context pipes: PHASE_EVAL + SWING_CONFIRMED on 3M/5M) — supersedes the prior "label-only rungs" characterization; **perception is all-TF, decision is single-TF.** (2) Measured cascade: decline labels 1M/3M 11:48 -> 5M 11:50 -> 15M 12:00; top-knowledge 1M 11:50 -> 3M 11:57 -> 5M 12:05 -> 15M 12:45 — k-lag scales linearly in wall-clock; **the signal TF learned the top 58 min late, 72% of the move spent**; trend-1 onset 1M 12:02 (aged 44) vs 15M 12:45. (3) The 12:27Z rv-5.99 climax is STRUCTURALLY NULL at 3M — aggregation deletes 1M events entirely, while decline texture (rv<1) is rung-invariant. (4) Filed under the H7 dossier + lab composition candidates (rung-consensus / ladder-trend-anchored H4-mirror variants) — round-1 material only; no engine or threshold implications now. (5) Six-rung note: rungs are full-perception TFs.

23. **Store-write race — discovered at commit time, fixed same day (2026-08-18).** (a) FINDING: `store.py build_one` truncate-rewrote every clean-store CSV in place (`df.to_csv` directly onto the target path), so any concurrent reader saw a truncated or empty file mid-build. Two real exposure classes: the git-snapshot race (OBSERVED instance: commit `9bcf9ec` captured `clean_finsa/uk100fut_1h.csv` as a 0-byte blob mid-rewrite; on-disk content was bit-identical to pre-commit within ~2 min; restored in `c3e806b`) and the graver live-reader race — a live loop warming from store during a rebuild reads truncated history, and warm state determines bin contents (register hazard rule; warm-anomaly cost is established precedent). (b) FIX: atomic writes in the store builder — `_atomic_to_csv` / `_atomic_json_dump` (temp file in the same directory + `os.replace`), covering all clean-store CSVs and `_report.json`. Pinned by `tests/test_store_atomic.py`: a crash at any point mid-write leaves the old file complete and unchanged; a reader opening at any moment sees old-or-new, never empty; temp files proven same-directory (same-filesystem atomicity). `*.tmp` gitignored against crash leftovers. (c) POLICY RECONCILIATION (stated decision): the README's 2026-08-14 "Excluded: raw/clean data stores and logs" was NEVER ENACTED — `.gitignore` exclusions sat commented out and stores/logs have been committed throughout. **README amended to match the enacted practice; the practice wins** because the ~30-day retention floor makes committed stores the only durable history — an untracked store loses everything beyond retention on the first mishap. The gitignore comment block now states tracking is deliberate. (d) HISTORY-SURGERY RULE ADDENDUM (extends register 20's rule): "stopped system + clean tree" now provably includes **no store builder running** — paper's per-poll persist path calls `build_one`, so a live paper process IS a store writer; kill order: processes first, then verify no build in flight (no `*.tmp` in clean_finsa/, stable mtimes), then surgery. Atomic writes narrow this race to the rename instant but the rule stands — surgery on a moving tree is the class, not the instance.

**Finding 23 annotation — the 2026-08-17 12:43Z rewrite adjudicated (2026-08-18; the "what did the surgery remove" question raised that day, answered now).** METHOD: pre-rewrite objects are unrecoverable (gc'd locally — old SHAs from the commit-map resolve to nothing; GitHub holds only post-rewrite objects — fetch-by-SHA refused "not our ref"), so the question was adjudicated from surviving evidence, not the filter's arguments — `.git/filter-repo/` archives maps only (commit-map = the archived logs/history_surgery copy, ref-map, first-changed-commits), not the invocation. **BRANCH (b) — STORE BLOBS WERE NOT STRIPPED, by direct observation:** pre-rewrite-dated commits still carry full store diffs (`4ad5468` "Refresh data" 2026-08-11 diffs clean_finsa/ files; `data/` and `clean_finsa/` blob history reaches back to `b0829e0` 2026-08-11); the untrack commit `12ded63`'s own message — "Past history retains earlier store snapshots" — remains TRUE post-rewrite. The committed-stores-durable-history doctrine was never violated. **What the rewrite did (INDICATED, NOT PROVEN — operator confirmation invited):** identity scrub. Evidence: the ROOT commit itself was rewritten (first-changed-commits `c85d0689`→`9dea94e6`) with every one of the 35 then-existing commits changed and no tree-level removal observable; surviving metadata shows exactly ONE author+committer identity across all history (the GitHub noreply address), zero traces of any other name/email in commit metadata; and the rewrite immediately preceded the repo's first push (remote advertises only post-rewrite objects; gitignore update + store re-add landed 6 min after the rewrite). A small path removal alongside cannot be excluded — absence is unobservable without the old objects — but no evidence points to one. **COROLLARY, added to the history-surgery rule (register 20, extended by finding 23c): store history is never filtered, ever** — adopted prophylactically per branch (b); the stores' git history is the only durable archive beyond the retention floor, and no future surgery objective (identity, secrets, size) may touch store paths. **COST OF DELIBERATE STORE TRACKING (priced, so tracking stays a decision, not a drift):** tracked stores+logs = 241.3 MB on disk (all tracked files 244.9 MB); measured append ≈ 3.3 MB/day raw (calendar-day basis across all store CSVs; ~100 MB/month, ~1.2 GB/yr raw). Git-side cost is lower — the full-history pack is 52 MB (~4.7:1 vs content; CSV appends delta-compress well; ~150 MB loose objects pending gc from the latest data commits). **Any future size pressure gets solved by anything EXCEPT filtering store history** — acceptable: partial/sparse clones for consumers, coarser data-commit cadence, archiving this repo intact and continuing in a successor — the filter is not on the menu.

24. **Watchdog calendar boundary — the pre-registered class from the Sunday runbook, now observed (2026-08-18).** (a) INCIDENT: 11 false WATCHDOG_STALL events 2026-08-17 21:11:33-22:03:01Z. `expect_prints` encoded the daily feed pause in LONDON hours (21:00-22:10 London); the provider pauses on UTC (DATA.md's ~22:00->~21:00 UTC day). One hour of BST offset = one hour of false alarms. MEASURED from tonight's store: last pre-pause bar opens 20:59Z (tape live to 21:00Z exactly), first post-pause bar 22:05Z; Sunday reopen 22:05Z corroborates. (b) FIX (`522a5cb`): calendar re-anchored to provider UTC — quiet window [21:00, 22:10) UTC (measured + 5 min reopen margin), weekend quiet Fri 21:00Z -> Sun 22:10Z (previously London-dayofweek). Pin test = the incident (tests/test_watchdog_calendar.py): the first and last false-event timestamps verbatim must be suppressed under the fixed calendar while a synthetic 5-quiet-poll stall at Monday 14:00Z still fires; measured boundaries and GMT-month behavior pinned. A STALE PIN CORRECTED (`b1acfe7`): tests/test_paper_watchdog.py asserted 20:30Z Friday is pause — it pinned the defect itself; store-measured tape prints to 20:59Z. (c) LEDGER: append-only NOTE added marking the 11 events as calendar-offset false positives, calendar fixed at 522a5cb. No restart urgency — false stalls are ledger noise, not risk; the fix rides the next natural restart, and until then the running process may print false stalls in the London-offset window. (d) FAMILY SWEEP (London-hours-where-provider-is-UTC): **store.py US/gold sessions — the DATA.md known sibling — ALREADY FIXED in code** (SESSION_TZ = America/New_York per instrument; comment records the old London hardcode was off by one hour in DST-divergence windows); narrate.py `in_cash` and loop.py EOD/embargo are EXCHANGE-anchored London — CORRECT for LSE instruments (the exchange, unlike the feed, genuinely lives on London wall time); sync/collect carry no time-window logic. NOTED, not a defect: `canonical_tod`'s 21:30-London trading-day anchor is an owner-ratified ENGINE convention shared by resampler and both live loops (consistency is the requirement, and it holds); seasonal nuance recorded — in BST months the anchor (20:30Z) sits in live pre-pause tape so the final ~30 pre-pause minutes bin into the next trading day, in GMT months it (21:30Z) sits inside the provider pause; session composition near the boundary therefore differs by season. Watched, no action. (e) SEASONAL DIMENSION, stated for the record: **the bug class is invisible in GMT months** — London == UTC and the false window closes on its own. A November observation that the stalls "stopped happening" would be the offset closing, NOT a fix. Any London-anchored provider-facing logic must be tested in BOTH offsets (the new pin does); this entry is the reference when a DST transition next changes live behavior unexplained.

25. **STANDING RULE — Evidence Regression (adopted 2026-08-18; run ad hoc three times this week, now mandatory).** Every defect finding carries a BLAST-RADIUS field before closure: (i) touched code paths enumerated; (ii) every evidence artifact class classified UNTOUCHED / TAINTED-RERUNNABLE / TAINTED-UNRECOVERABLE — **and test assertions count as evidence artifacts:** the sweep includes grepping existing tests for pins derived from the defective belief (motivating instance: tests/test_paper_watchdog.py's 20:30Z assertion ENCODED finding 24's bug — the suite was green because it agreed with the defect); (iii) rerunnable artifacts get rerun-and-diff on fixed code, diff archived, confirmations logged as evidence; (iv) unrecoverable spans get honestly-scoped caveats per the canonicality precedent (tod-defect pre-fix window); (v) any downstream decision fed by an amended result is explicitly re-adjudicated — kills, GOs, prioritizations — by walking the trial-log chain; (vi) lockbox discipline is never suspended for a rerun. COROLLARIES: **(a) engine commit hash mandatory on all lab/census artifacts.** Retroactive verification (2026-08-18): T3 records its engine state (boundary_check.head 10aabb60); T1/T1b/T1c/T1d artifacts record NONE — gap annotated in the trial log; mitigation: each census was committed atomically with the code state that produced it, so the engine state is recoverable as the introducing commit (T1=12e1d75, T1b=74a0536, T1c=0376744, T1d=1c02853); recoverable-by-parentage is acceptable for the four legacy artifacts, in-artifact hash is mandatory from now on. **(b) Pin provenance mandatory** — every incident-pin test states its evidence source (measured artifact vs spec-derived); a pin whose provenance is a belief is the finding-24 failure mode. **(c) Test invocations must propagate exit codes** — no tail/pipe masking; pipefail or direct invocation (motivating instance: 522a5cb committed on an unseen red — `pytest | tail` returned tail's exit status).

**Finding 24 amendment — Evidence Regression applied, first formal instance (2026-08-18).** BLAST RADIUS: touched code = `expect_prints` alone, consumed ONLY by the live stall alarm (paper.py watchdog guard); it feeds no bars, bins, labels, features, or replay paths. EVIDENCE CLASSES: all lab-cycle artifacts (T1/T1b/T1c/T1d/T3), event studies, campaign reports, regrade/signature captures — UNTOUCHED (no data path through the defect); ledger — TAINTED-RERUNNABLE at the annotation level: 11 false WATCHDOG_STALL events, cured by the append-only NOTE (the events stand as honest history, reclassified); test suite — one TAINTED pin (test_paper_watchdog.py 20:30Z), cured `b1acfe7`. DOWNSTREAM DECISIONS: none — no kill, GO, or prioritization consumed a watchdog event; trial-log chain walked, no re-adjudication required. THE WEEK'S THREE INFORMAL PRECEDENTS this rule formalizes: (1) tod-defect canonicality caveat (all pre-fix live label events caveated, settled replay canonical); (2) comparator TEST-blindness re-cut (presence study re-run symmetric, 24/24, "10:26Z label-less" falsified); (3) T1d pooled->matched re-cut (census defect, matched-only cell recomputed, gate re-adjudicated and HELD). DEPLOYMENT: the running paper process still carries the London calendar; **the calendar fix deploys at the next natural restart — item 22's session-JSONL deployment restart is the designated vehicle; until then the nightly ~21:10-22:03Z false stalls (BST offset window) are EXPECTED and covered by the ledger NOTE** — do not re-investigate them. DATA.md's US/gold "fix before NASDAQ stage" note amended to fixed-at-commit (`6115e8a`, SESSION_TZ) per the finding-24 family sweep.

Convening the narrative-exit values discussion is the OPERATOR'S call on which reading the gate meant; any flip-exit rule would in any case need tail protection (e.g. flip-exit only before progress arms, staged grammar already expresses this). No recipe changes without ratification.

47. **Scoreboard fairness amendment — class-conditioned chance (operator order 2026-08-20).** RULE: for any hypothesis whose firing condition conditions on a bar-size/volatility class, the scoreboard computes the class's own chance rate per context, prints it beside the whole-window rate, and grades the row's lift against the CONDITIONED one. **Unconditioned lift on a conditioned signal is a known confound class; the scoreboard refuses to display it as headline lift.** Declarations live in the signal module (CONDITIONED_ROWS, one-home rule); mask implementations in the scoreboard, name-keyed; matrix and card cells show "[cls-chance X% vs uncond Y%]" and mark/grade from the conditioned rate. APPLIED TO S-H6 (class = its own ratified p90-width condition over the trailing day window) and re-emitted: wide bars alone qualify at 37.8% (backtest) vs 23.6% unconditioned — the volatility confound explained ~14pp of yesterday's +22pp headline; HONEST CONDITIONED LIFT: +8.0pp backtest (27/59), +11.5pp forward (6/11, small-n). Above its class's chance at modest n; keep-watching stands; the register-46 review question is RESOLVED into this standing column.

60. **Baselines + the H1 harvest program + the enforceable glossary (operator + reviewer, 2026-08-22).** (1) THREE BASELINE PSEUDO-SIGNALS pre-registered (prereg_baselines, counted) and built through the IDENTICAL harvest machinery (ladder, sessions, spread, honest fills, one-position), every instrument, both windows: B-TREND (with-1M-trend fires every 60 bars — cadence FLAGGED implementer-proposed — trend_age gated by the same ESTABLISHED_TREND_AGE the trend family fires on), B-RANDOM (uniform random timing/direction, seed 60, PINNED), B-ALWAYS-LONG (periodic longs — the drift reference). CONTROLS, NEVER CANDIDATES: fenced BASELINES section, permanently excluded from hypothesis counts, labels, and promotion machinery (fence stated in the artifact and pinned). SIGNAL_POINTS gains the matched-baseline column (Δmed@3×): trend family (S0-H10, S0-H14 — by DERIVATION, the only established-trend-gated firing conditions) vs B-TREND; long-skewed (≥0.8 long share, rule FLAGGED) vs B-ALWAYS-LONG; else vs B-RANDOM — the subtraction answering "does the hypothesis add anything beyond exposure?" (2) H1 HARVEST PROGRAM pre-registered as a counted experiment family against S0-H1's FIXED fires, the signal untouched — six trial-log entries: entry-delay 3 bars, entry-delay 10 bars, first-confirming-print entry (founding sequence), own-curve geometry (stop = measured MAE p90 per instrument, time-exit = time-to-MFE median, values resolved from the excursion artifacts at build and cited, never re-tuned), session-conditioned harvest read (existing matrix machinery), and a falling-knife early-exit stage (flip-guard pattern, staged grammar, arming per existing primitives). Graded on net and median vs BOTH S0-H1's current cells AND the matched baseline; forward accrual decides, backtest is design-input only. TEMPLATE CLAUSE: if this moves H1's median from negative toward flat-plus, the same family runs for the next precision-points-gap signal. Build slot queued (backlog). (3) docs/GLOSSARY.md built — one plain-English line per term, grouped (measurement / structure / object families / label anatomy / machinery / process / incidentals) — and made ENFORCEABLE: tests/test_glossary.py sweeps the generated reports, the register, and docs for uppercase terms; a term neither defined in the glossary nor plain English fails the suite (seed offenders MAE, MFE, ND/NS, PoC, S-numbers, Q-numbers, R-/B- prefixes asserted unconditionally). New terms enter the glossary in the same commit that first uses them, or the commit doesn't pass. Every generated report header links the glossary; README reading order gains it as item 2.

61. **Baseline ensemble upgrade (operator, 2026-08-22).** The floor becomes a DISTRIBUTION, not a draw (prereg_baselines_v2_ensemble, counted): B-RANDOM runs as a 20-seed ensemble (seeds 60–79, pinned); B-TREND runs as 20 cadence phase-offsets (same 60-bar interval, shifted starts 0,3,…,57, pinned); B-ALWAYS-LONG stays deterministic (single member, degenerate range). Every baseline cell reports the ensemble MEDIAN with the min–max member range beside it; the matched-baseline column compares signals against the ensemble median and prints the floor's range beside the delta — an "above baseline" claim is visibly inside or outside the floor's own noise. Ensemble membership counts and measured-member counts carried in every cell (n = median member n). SINGLE-SEED HISTORY ARCHIVED, SUPERSEDED: the register-60 single-draw artifacts remain in git history (commits 773f623/0d11265); the registry authority and pins now name BASELINE_SEEDS/TREND_OFFSETS. Cadence (60 bars) and the drift-skew match rule (0.8) remain FLAGGED implementer-proposed, unchanged by this upgrade.

62. **DOCTRINE — edge is consistency (operator + reviewer, 2026-08-22; registered and wired into the pending governance criteria).** (1) PRINCIPLE REGISTERED: edge = catching moves before they begin, harvested such that the TYPICAL trade wins — not tail-carried nets requiring deep pockets. Assessment priority, formally: (i) INITIATION lift vs conditioned chance; (ii) MEDIAN-PER-TRADE at widths ≤3×ATR(15M); (iii) VS-BASELINE CLEARANCE outside the ensemble's FULL member range (above the floor's luck, not just its median). Tail-carried cells (positive net, negative median) are explicitly NON-EVIDENCE for promotion or labels, at any n. (2) The pending label-criteria proposal (register 36; promote/deprioritize numbers awaiting ratification) REDRAFTED on these three axes in the registry (review_promote_min_fires / review_deprioritize_min_fires entries) and re-flagged for the governance sitting — the doctrine defines the criteria's SHAPE; the sitting ratifies the NUMBERS. (3) The baseline framework annotated with the doctrine's reading (SIGNAL_POINTS header): the floors are the deep-pockets return made explicit; beating them is the operational meaning of "edge." (4) DASHBOARD BOARD REORDERED to doctrine priority: initiation lift first, S0 median@3× forward second, baseline clearance (vs floor MAX member) third, net demoted to a context column. NOTHING RE-GRADES: this changes what gets looked at; the governance sitting changes what gets acted on.

63. **Bar-persistence census pre-registered and built (operator + reviewer, 2026-08-22; prereg_bar_persistence, counted).** SCALP-VIABILITY MEASUREMENT, NO STRATEGY (backtest/bar_persistence.py → bar_persistence.md|.json, weekly under Part C): for every registered signal's fires, over N ∈ {1,3,5,10} settled 1M bars from next-open entry — (a) favorable-close fraction (signed by fire direction, vs entry); (b) reach probabilities at +{2,5,10} points (HOME-REFERENCE ONLY) and +{0.25,0.5,1.0}×ATR(15M) (cross-instrument); (c) time-to-first-touch distribution; (d) the same reach probabilities NET of measured spread — the scalp-economics column (reach counts only past entry+spread); (e) every cell against its matched baseline ensemble (register 60/61 matching; B-RANDOM's own bar-persistence is the floor — random entries also see favorable bars; floors computed for every scope the signals report, whole + sessions). Per signal × instrument × session, both windows; counts everywhere; dimming and PROVISIONAL standard; sealed/lockbox excluded; twin-run + spread-netting + instrumentation pinned (tests/test_bar_persistence.py). HEADER STATES: measurement for assessment; any scalp recipe is a separate counted registration, gated on cells that clear spread AND baseline and survive forward accrual. EXPECTED OUTCOME, STATED HONESTLY: this census will likely nominate DIFFERENT signals than register 62's consistency screen — high-persistence/short-horizon signals may be poor initiators; if so, both facts print side by side, and the scalp question becomes its own thread with its own candidates rather than being absorbed into the doctrine's ranking. First emission run with the current windows.

64. **The scalp question + the expectancy curve + process hygiene (operator + reviewer, 2026-08-23).** (1) **Q2-H6 MINTED** (prereg_Q2-H6, counted) — the scalp thread's first question, through the census entry's own gate: PREDICTION — S0-H6's net 0.5×ATR/5-bar reach (33.9% backtest, +22.8pp above B-RANDOM's floor median, clear of its full member range [9.4…15.0]) persists in forward accrual ABOVE its matched floor's full member range; grade at ≥30 forward fires (home; away cells post-validation), census conventions (net-of-spread, ensemble floors). NO SCALP RECIPE registers unless it grades YES. The scalp thread's candidate list opens with this question and takes nominations ONLY from census cells that clear spread, floor range, and forward accrual — the same front door as everything. (2) **CENSUS EXTENDED — the per-bar expectancy curve** (points at bar+k is the design quantity): for every registered signal's fires, signed points at the close of bar+k, k ∈ {1…10}, reported CUMULATIVE (entry→bar+k close: mean, median, n) and INCREMENTAL (bar+k's own contribution: mean, median), both gross and net-of-spread (spread charged ONCE at entry — only bar+1's increment nets it, pinned); same splits (signal × instrument × session, both windows), same ensemble floors (B-RANDOM's own per-bar curve is the null — random entries drift too), counts and dimming standard, twin-run pinned. Rendering: md prints cumulative net medians at k ∈ {1,3,5,10} plus peak-k (where the cumulative median curve tops out); full curves in the JSON. PURPOSE stated in the header: peak-k and the incremental sign-flip bar are the mechanical inputs a scalp recipe's time-exit would cite; no recipe without its question grading YES, per (1). (3) **BACKGROUND-PROCESS HYGIENE — standing practice:** background fallback loops must always CHECK-AND-STOP as today's did (task bb7tsbjg5: fired stale, verified completion against the record, stopped, tree clean); any work a fallback would perform rides the same commit-and-register discipline as the main flow. A fallback that can write unsupervised is a second author; THE RECORD HAS ONE PEN.

**Register 64 amendment — scalp-thread design note (operator, 2026-08-23; logged, not a silent edit):** if Q2-H6 grades YES, any resulting recipe must be TARGET-BASED — a resting order at the reach level; touch = fill, with the standard queue-risk optimism stated in the recipe's header. The TIME-EXIT variant is already RULED OUT by the expectancy curve (S0-H6 cumulative net median non-positive at all k; peak-k=2 at −0.10): the touch column and the expectancy column nominate DIFFERENT harvest mechanics by construction — wick-based reach pays a resting order, close-based expectancy pays a time-exit — and recipes cite which column they live on.

65. **Fill-model asymmetry — standing fact, not a per-recipe footnote (operator, 2026-08-23).** The HARVEST-LADDER family (recipes, stop-width ladder, R2 experiment, the MAE/stop side of excursion geometry) prices stops PESSIMISTICALLY: always fill against you (register 41 rules — stop wins ties, gaps fill worse). Any TOUCH=FILL family (the census reach columns; the TP-ladder side of excursion geometry; any future target-based scalp recipe) prices targets OPTIMISTICALLY: always fill for you. STANDING REQUIREMENTS: (a) every artifact header states its family's fill model; (b) any page rendering both families carries the non-comparability line (bar_persistence, excursion_geometry, and the dashboard board note wired this entry); (c) the glossary gains "fill model (pessimistic / optimistic)"; (d) when a real scalp recipe registers, its optimism gets a STATED HAIRCUT ASSUMPTION (e.g., touch must exceed the level by one tick to count as filled) — proposed at registration, operator-ratified like everything (Q2-H6's IF-YES clause updated). Template headers carry the lines from this commit; artifacts print them at their next Part C emission.

69. **THE NARRATIVE DOCTRINE — the project's organizing principle (operator + reviewer, 2026-08-23; alongside register 62).** The edge hypothesis of this project is that MARKET NARRATIVE — multiple hypotheses in sequence and context, telling a story — carries predictive and harvestable structure that isolated signals do not. Atomic hypotheses (H1–H16) are the tested VOCABULARY; the narrative layer (conditioning, sequels, composites like H13/H15) is where the thesis actually lives. CONSEQUENCES, RECORDED: (1) atomic signals reading at-floor is CONSISTENT with the thesis, not against it — the null being tested was never "climaxes alone pay"; (2) the measurement roadmap's second phase is NARRATIVE INSTRUMENTS — the sequel census (first, register 71), sequence-grammar extensions of the conditioning matrix (event-ORDER dimensions, not just state dimensions), and composite minting through the front door as sentences prove out; (3) THE DOCTRINE CUTS BOTH WAYS AND SAYS SO: if sentences also read at-floor as their measurements mature, the narrative thesis itself is being falsified — and that verdict is accepted on the same terms as any atom's. The register exists to grade the story, not to protect it.

70. **R2 VERDICT-READ (operator ruling, 2026-08-23; all cited numbers verified against r2_experiment.json this entry).** The founding refinement question is ANSWERED on the counted experiment: median entry improvement negative across nearly all rows — the micro-loop does not improve entries on average; spread ≈29% of exec-local stops on the flagship row (S0-H1 28.9%), as the founding worry stated. BUT THE NET EFFECT SPLITS BY FAMILY: climax/trend rows WORSEN under refinement (S0-H1 backtest −76.8 unrefined → −139.5 refined); rejection/absorption rows IMPROVE (S0-H6 forward −10.9 → +37.5 — the only green home forward cell; S0-H9 backtest −63.2 → −9.7; S1-H11 backtest −147.3 → −66.1; S1-H2 backtest −108.3 → −60.6). RULING REGISTERED: refinement is a PER-FAMILY DESIGN CHOICE, never universal; every future recipe registration states refined/unrefined as a CITED decision (this entry is the citation root). Mechanistic reading consistent with the anatomy: reversal-confirmation entries (close-position trigger) cost the climax family its early fills but filter the rejection family's knives.

72. **Deep-dive follow-through — three amendments (operator + reviewer, 2026-08-23).** (a) H1 HARVEST PROGRAM AMENDED per R2 (register 70) and the sequel census (register 71): the refinement-flavored arm (prereg_H1_harvest_confirm, first-confirming-print entry) is DROPPED — R2 shows refinement worsens the climax family (logged trial-log amendment, not a silent edit); a NAS100 ARM is added (prereg_H1_harvest_nas100, counted), ACTIVATING POST-VALIDATION — the +15.8pp at n=35 cell is the program's best target; the entry-delay arm gains a CONDITIONAL VARIANT contingent on the sequel census — if sequel class separates winners from knives, delay becomes "enter on the absorption sequel, stand aside on opposing effort," registered THEN as its own counted variant, not now. (b) Q2-H6 CONTINGENT PREP (drafted here; REGISTERS ONLY ON A YES GRADE; nothing computes until the grade lands): target-based recipe skeleton — resting order at the 0.5×ATR reach level; haircut proposal per register 65(d): touch must exceed the level by ONE TICK to count as filled (home: 0.5 pts, uk100fut tick_size cited from config; per-instrument tick at registration); refinement-interaction NOTED as a design input (register 70: refinement rescues H6's family — the refined variant is a legitimate arm IF the question grades YES). (c) REGISTER-NOTE — the home board's honest baseline reading of 2026-08-23: under INITIATION grading, every home hypothesis currently sits inside its floor's noise. 73. **The narrative survey census — the doctrine's systematic instrument (operator + reviewer, 2026-08-23; prereg_narrative_survey, counted). Q2-H1 NOT MINTED — the proposed sequel-cell question is SUPERSEDED by this survey before ever registering; the H1-absorption cell enters as the survey's focal cell instead.** (1) REGISTERED SEQUEL VOCABULARY — the sentence alphabet, from the existing event stream only, definitions cited, no new thresholds: absorption-class (S0-H3 signature / narrow-loud quadrant bar [register 66] / qualifying TEST print), opposing-effort (continued wide-loud in the anchor bar's own close direction), same-direction effortless move (classifier EFFORTLESS labels in the fire's direction), trend-flip (either direction, TF STATED: 1M), node/gap arrival (H11's ratified map; class change from the anchor bar), no-sequel. Precedence stated: first qualifying bar; within a bar absorption > opposing > effortless-same > trend-flip > map-arrival. (2) THE FULL GRID: every signal-live anchor × every sequel class × W ∈ {5,15} — initiation lift and payoff medians per cell, class-conditioned floors (register-47 method), counts, dimming, both windows, all instruments, PROVISIONAL carried, sealed/lockbox excluded, twin-run pinned, weekly under Part C. The two-anchor sequel census (register 71) is RECORDED AS THIS SURVEY'S PILOT; its machinery generalizes; the campaign slot passes to the survey. (3) FIELD-SIZE HONESTY, MANDATORY: the header states the total cell count; every emission computes the EXPECTED-MAXIMUM-UNDER-NULL — permutation of sequel labels within anchor (100 shuffles, pinned seed set 0–99; label permutation preserves each cell's n; bases are bar-population properties and do not reshuffle) — a cell is SURVEY-NOTABLE only if its |lift| exceeds the null's expected max among measurable cells; the page prints null-predicted glow count vs actual, so the field's luck budget is always visible. (4) THE S0-H1-ABSORPTION CELL keeps its earned status: the survey's ONE pre-registered focal cell — mechanism and prediction stated before the survey existed (register 71's first emission; the founding two-chapter story) — head start real and cited, but it GRADES WITHIN THE FIELD, against the same null, no special treatment in the arithmetic. (5) Q-MINTING RULE: a cell becomes a question only if SURVEY-NOTABLE and carrying a WRITTEN MECHANISM SENTENCE — no mechanism, no clock, however bright. The H1 program's sequel-contingent delay variant chains on a future YES of whatever question the focal cell earns (register 72 spirit unchanged). (6) SCOPE DISCIPLINE: two-clause sentences only in v1 (anchor + one sequel); three-clause sentences gate behind two-clause survivors — the H12/H15 starvation precedent cited. PURPOSE: this is the narrative doctrine's THOROUGHNESS instrument — the systematic survey of the sentence space, with its own luck fully priced, per the operator's requirement that the thesis be INVESTIGATED, not anecdotally confirmed. If the field glows only at null-expected rates at current data depth, that reading stresses the doctrine itself, per register 69 clause (3).

**Register 73 amendment — the consistency statistic (operator + reviewer, 2026-08-23; logged, not a silent edit).** The survey gains a SECOND permutation test over the SAME pinned shuffles — the doctrine's actual prediction shape: for each label-shuffle, count anchor×sequel PAIRS with ≥3 measurable cells (n≥15) whose lifts are same-signed at |lift|>3pp in ALL cells; the null distribution of that count prints against the actual. A pair is CONSISTENCY-NOTABLE if the actual count's field percentile exceeds 95 AND the pair itself is same-signed across windows. Both statistics print side by side (extremity + consistency); the notable+mechanism minting rule now accepts EITHER route. **REVIEWER ERROR RECORDED (secondhand-summary strike, logged):** the first emission's "field reads at null-expected rates" summary was the EXTREMITY statistic overgeneralized into a field verdict — extremity asks whether any single cell is impossibly bright; the doctrine predicts many moderately-bright same-signed cells, which extremity cannot see. The operator's read of the artifact caught it. Constants (3 cells / n≥15 / 3pp / p95) operator-stated in the amendment, registered in the AUTHORITY map.

74. **Findings audit — every registered finding re-derived from its artifact (operator-ordered, 2026-08-23; full table: audit/findings_audit_2026-08-23.md).** 22 finding-bearing rows inventoried (claims, questions, labels, verdict-reads, standing recommendations — the graded/negative ones included), three checks each: (a) re-derivation, (b) statistic-shape match, (c) negative-finding symmetry. **Register 74-C — interpretation-layer guards (operator, 2026-08-23; from the audit's verdict: results were true, interpretation bent).** (1) VERDICT SENTENCES USE REGISTERED QUANTIFIERS: "every measurable," "all cells at n≥X" — never bare universals (the A1 class). (2) **COMPARISON ENGINE** (reports/forensics/, one md per market + cross-market + JSON): winners-vs-losers categorical splits per instrument x sentence x variant x session against (a) the cell's class-conditioned base, (b) a permutation null — win/loss labels shuffled within sentence x variant strata, 100 pinned seeds, cell set fixed at observed measurability, luck budget printed per market — with per-cell survivor bar |split| >= 10pp AND clearing the cell's own null p95; (c) counts and dimming everywhere (measurable = wins+losses >= 25, each side >= 10; scratches excluded from labels). 87. **The underwater census + thesis-campaign study — loser-cutting and the operator's trading loop, pre-registered (operator + reviewer, 2026-08-28; prereg_underwater_census + prereg_thesis_campaign, counted; backtest/underwater_census.py -> underwater_census.md|.json + thesis_campaign.md|.json, weekly under Part C after first emission).** THE MOTIVATING POLICY, RECORDED: the operator's manual loop as relayed in the order — hold a thesis; probe it with fires that agree; move stops to breakeven fast; re-enter on the next setup; the thesis dies when the state flips; back off after consecutive refusals (verbatim source = the order text of 2026-08-28; drift from the operator's own wording correctable at the sitting). R-BE's scratch-machine record (register 81 note) and the register-42d breakeven literature caution stand as OPPOSING evidence; the census arbitrates; the flip-cut precedent (register 44) governs — measurement before machinery. (1) **PART 1, where losses live:** per-trade bars-to-first-positive-close, MAE at bars 1..10 xATR, register-66 quadrant per underwater bar, path classes (winner-clean / winner-after-underwater / loser-never-positive / loser-after-positive; scratches apart); (a) four-class loss decomposition (the early-cut theoretical maximum = the never-positive share, stated as such); (b) survival curves P(win | underwater at k) with n at every k — the operator hypothesis "winners become positive within 2 bars" RECORDED and graded by the curve, never assumed; (c) the discrimination frontier (separation vs residual avoidable points by k); (d) quiet-dip vs loud-dip conditional win rates (the in-trade reading stated as motivation: a quiet dip is the story breathing, a loud dip is the story changing — the numbers decide); (e) the counterfactual rule ladder (cut@{2,3,5} bars underwater close-fill; cut-on-first-loud-underwater-close; cut@MAE>{0.5,1.0}xATR touch-fill — OPTIMISTIC class, in-ladder comparisons only, register-65 declarations) with B-RANDOM member floors under identical rules and the label-permutation luck budget (100 pinned seeds; field statistics at the declared reference bar k=3). (2) **PART 2, the BE ladder + re-entry:** BE at +{0.25,0.5,1.0,1.5}xATR MFE on the R-NOTRAIL base (settled arming, entry-touch fill); per rung: scratches created / winners amputated / losses capped / net vs actual with member floors; the NEVER-AGAIN table — max MFE of eventual losers per rung, naming which rung makes the +216-class structurally impossible and its amputation cost; (g) re-entry measured on the UNDEDUPED fire stream (the 5-bar dedup hides near re-fires — the interaction stated) with same-sentence re-fire rates at {15,30,60} min. (3) **PART 3, the thesis-campaign study** (artifact named THESIS-CAMPAIGN — the word "campaign" collides with the weekly pipeline, stated): thesis v1 = the 15M phase-tracker trend state (existing instrument, no new thresholds; v2 candidate sentence-family states RECORDED, NOT BUILT); invalidation = the state flip (the NEXIT-SENT opposing-class vocabulary is the detector family); probes = agreeing journal fires, one position at a time, stop 2.5xATR, BE at the rung grid, re-entry on the next agreeing fire; campaigns intersecting the lockbox span DROPPED and counted (register 85); MANDATORY random-thesis controls — 20 pinned members (seeds range(60,80), the B-RANDOM lineage) on the SAME campaign windows (sticky random theses in a trending month are the stated confound); (iii) the attribution ladder one-shot -> +BE -> +re-entry, declared a POLICY LADDER, not a causal claim; (iv) the backoff grid k in {2,3} x W in {5,15,30} bars plus resume-on-confirming-print (a fresh effortless-same print in the thesis direction, the register-73 class) plus no-backoff, at the operator's fast rung 0.25 (implementer choice, flagged; other rungs' grids a flagged follow-up), with the ABSTAINED COUNTERFACTUAL (losses avoided AND winners missed in suppression windows — backoff's price) mandatory; per-campaign risk accounting in every table (the per-trade view is the known flattery); consecutive scratches recorded as a candidate narrative event ("repeated refusal") — if resume-on-print dominates the timers, that finding routes to the front door as its own question. (4) **FENCES:** ex-lockbox throughout; both windows; all four instruments; sealed skipped and counted; zone reconciliation, fill declarations, and luck budgets in every header; twin-run pinned (tests/test_underwater_census.py); statistic shapes per 74-B; the census's array-walk simulator reconciles against the journal's R-NOTRAIL rows (drift stated); NOTHING in this study changes any live recipe, label, or signal; every resulting candidate rule needs its own Q-number and forward grade.

**Register 87 first-emission note (statistic shapes as declared per section; quantifiers verified against underwater_census.json and thesis_campaign.json; 2026-08-28 run).** (1) **THE EARLY-CUT HEADROOM IS STRUCTURALLY SMALL:** the loser-never-positive class holds 2-23% of total loss points per measurable family (mostly under 15%) — the majority of losses come from trades that WERE positive at some point. The money the operator wants back sits in the GIVE-BACK class (breakeven/trail territory), not the cut@k class. (2) **THE OPERATOR HYPOTHESIS GRADES PARTIAL:** winners positive within 2 bars run 0.47-0.89 by family (mostly 0.55-0.80) — a majority, never near-all; a naive 2-bar cut amputates a 20-45% winner minority, and the rule-ladder nets price exactly that. (3) **THE VSA IN-TRADE QUESTION FAILS ITS LUCK BUDGET at current depth:** quiet-vs-loud dip discrimination at the reference bar clears nothing (0-1 qualifying cells vs null p95 across all four instruments; the one uk100fut separation cell at percentile 98 is a single-market glimmer, listed not minted). (4) **THE BE LADDER SPLITS BY CONTINENT:** at the operator's fast rung 0.25, net delta is POSITIVE on the EU legs (uk100fut +1,995, ger40fut +906) and STRONGLY NEGATIVE on the US legs (nas100fut -4,946, us30fut -10,854) — amputation dwarfs capping where the tape runs; at rung 1.5 the spread narrows (uk +1,135 / ger +226 / nas +705 / us30 -3,625). NEVER-AGAIN CAVEAT, recorded: the structural bound (loser MFE < rung) holds everywhere EXCEPT the single-bar span case — a bar wide enough to arm the rung AND hit the 2.5xATR stop defeats settled arming (observed: us30fut rung 1.5, a 2.533xATR/123.6pt loser). Fast rungs held the bound in-sample; the exception is the honest print. (5) **RE-ENTRY SUPPLY EXISTS:** same-sentence re-fires follow 11-20% of fast-BE scratches within 15 min and 31-35% within 60 min (undeduped stream — the 5-bar dedup hides part of this from the journal). (6) **THE THESIS-CAMPAIGN VERDICT IS MOSTLY NEGATIVE FOR THE LOOP AS PRACTICED:** at rung 0.25 the full loop underperforms its random-thesis member-band median on three of four legs (ger40fut -157.7 vs -41.4; uk100fut -153.7 vs -64.9; us30fut -513.0 vs +100.8) and BEATS it only on nas100fut (+728.5 vs +463, near band max 870); the attribution ladder shows one-shot-no-BE beating the full loop on the same three legs (uk100fut +55.6 vs -153.7 the sharpest) — on this journal, fast-BE-plus-re-entry SUBTRACTS from simply taking the first agreeing probe, except on nas100fut. (7) **BACKOFF:** timers trim losses modestly (best rows k=2/W=30) and resume-on-print does NOT dominate — worst or near-worst on every leg — so no front-door routing triggers; the abstained counterfactual prints (losses avoided vs winners missed) and no backoff variant flips any leg's sign. Campaigns dropped for lockbox intersection: 33-40 per leg, counted. ALL OF IT CANDIDATES AND TEXTURE — no rule, rung, or backoff registers from this entry; the sitting reads the curves.

PER RUNG: zones per leg, active-at-once (max concurrent over the 1M span), in-zone bar fraction, in-zone fires, pooled in-zone initiation vs the zone-conditioned base — THE DENSITY-vs-LIFT CURVE, so "significant and recent" gets its mechanical values from the table rather than by fiat; per-signal cells per rung in the JSON, location family marked. VERDICT TEXTURE, no decision: "significant and recent" has its mechanical values now, and at none of them do H4 reversal zones condition initiation on this store; NOTHING MINTS; the ladder accrues weekly under Part C, the null anchor stands.

WATCH-ONLY, recorded, no chain: the ger40fut S0-H19 forward net reach 21.4% vs its floor max 18.7 (n=126) — one cell of twelve, luck-plausible at that margin, no question minted.

100. **The H6 refinement program — a counted experiment family on the scalp thread (operator-commissioned, 2026-08-28; prereg_h6_refinement_program, counted before any computation; backtest/h6_refinement.py → h6_refinement.md|.json, weekly under Part C).** v0 (R-SCALP-H6, register 94) UNTOUCHED; every output is a CANDIDATE ARM through the front door only — surviving candidates → ratification → v1 arms, forward-graded; the September sealed window examines whatever registers. (a) CO-FIRE CONTEXT CENSUS: per S0-H6 fire, every other signal firing within ±5 bars (the fires.csv self-join on the 1M axis); class = with-S vs without-S per co-firing signal plus any-vs-none; outcome = the net 0.5×ATR/5-bar reach (registers 63/64); split in pp against a touch-label permutation null within instrument × window strata (100 pinned seeds) — per-cell null p95 and the field count vs null as the luck budget; both windows, all four instruments, no pooling. (b) DISCRIMINATOR RULE: candidate-grade only if |split| ≥ 10pp AND beyond its own null p95, same sign, on ≥ 3 of 4 instruments (windows pooled per leg for the rule; single-window splits printed); one- and two-leg glints LISTED, never chained. (c) VETO COUNTERFACTUALS for candidate-grade classes: filtered vs unfiltered book on IDENTICAL fires — reach, net on the census's scalp economics (touch → +0.5×ATR15(fire) − spread; non-touch → cumulative close at bar 5 − spread; the per-fire ledger bar_persistence_fires.csv gains atr15_at_fire and cum_pts_bar{3,5,8,10} columns this entry), rows retained, amputation cost (net-touchers dropped); floors = the instrument's B-RANDOM member range (a time-matched filtered floor is a FLAGGED follow-up). (d) TIME-CAP DERIVATION: P(touch by 5 | dry at k), k=1..4, per instrument with n at every k; exit-at-k ∈ {3,5,8} for non-reachers vs the 10-bar-horizon hold (the census ruler — a PROXY for EOD-flat, stated; the true comparison comes from the scalp arms' journal rows), net of spread; register 64's time-exit ruling CITED and DISTINGUISHED — the target is retained, only the non-reach tail is capped; any cap proposes JOINTLY with the v1 price-stop from the same forward non-reach accrual. (e) SCOPE FENCE: the cell budget printed in the header (features × instruments × windows; measurable count); enrichment-column cuts (session, quadrant, MTF joined at the fire from trades_enriched) held to the same ≥ 3-of-4 rule; nothing modifies v0; twin-run pinned (tests/test_h6_refinement.py — tagger window and exclusions, scalp economics, the 3-of-4 rule on a synthetic separator, the time-cap curve, luck-budget determinism).

And capping non-reachers at bar 3 BEATS the 10-bar hold on ALL FOUR legs (net delta cap-minus-hold, n non-reachers): uk100fut +44.5 (75), ger40fut +91.2 (70), nas100fut +435.1 (60), us30fut +557.7 (73) — the non-reach tail bleeds through the horizon, widest on the US legs; cap@5 and cap@8 are mixed (ger40fut negative at both). 101. **Scalp refinement — the time-cap on its real basis (operator + reviewer, 2026-08-28; AMENDMENT_h6_refinement_real_basis, counted before computation).** (1) THE 10-BAR PROXY IS REPLACED: exit-at-k ∈ {3,5,8} is now priced against the scalp arm's ACTUAL journal outcome on the same fires (R-SCALP-H6 plain-arm rows from trades_enriched.csv joined to the per-fire ledger at fire = open_ts − 1M), net of spread, per instrument, both windows, ex-lockbox. THE BASIS CORRECTION THE ROWS FORCED (74-C): the journal target has NO TIME LIMIT — 82% of scalp rows eventually reach — so a cap at bar k applies to every row still DRY at bar k, and its price has THREE parts: late-reach AMPUTATION (targets that would have hit after bar k), eod savings (drift), and stop savings (disaster) — the proxy read (register 100 note) counted only eventual non-reachers and is superseded as the cap's evidence base. (2) DECOMPOSITION by the avoided tail's exit reason printed per cell; stops in the dry set exit after bar k and are dodged by construction; stops at or before bar k are counted apart as not dodgeable — that is the direct answer to "does cap@3 dodge the stop-cluster class". The referenced week-32 forensic (36 full-ATR stops, most with MFE ≤ 55) is re-verified on the scalp rows with its lockbox overlap stated (ISO week 32 straddles the excluded span from 2026-08-04; only pre-boundary rows count). (3) THE JOINT v1 PROPOSAL emits ONLY if cap@3 holds on the real basis on ≥ 3 forward legs (holds = net delta > 0 at n ≥ 10 dry rows): bar-3 cap (target retained; register 64 distinguished) + a tighter price-stop DERIVED from the forward non-reach distribution — candidate = the p75 of forward non-reachers' MAE (×ATR15 at fire) per instrument, p50/p90 printed beside, with its amputation cost = the share of forward reachers whose MAE exceeded it — ONE RATIFICATION PACKAGE, numbers with derivations attached, NOTHING REGISTERED until the operator rules. Pinned (tests/test_h6_refinement.py: the dry-at-k decomposition on a synthetic five-row case).

**Register 101 first-emission note (2026-08-28 run; quantifiers verified against h6_refinement.json; 302 scalp rows ex-lockbox).** THE REAL BASIS REVERSES THE PROXY READ — register 100's "cap@3 beats the hold on all four legs" is SUPERSEDED: the 10-bar proxy ignored the late reachers, and they dominate. Cap@3 is net NEGATIVE on 6 of 8 instrument × window cells (uk100fut −78.2 backtest / −102.5 forward; ger40fut −57.3 / +13.0; nas100fut −262.7 / −57.6; us30fut +393.1 / −350.9) — it holds on ger40fut forward (+13.0, n=36 dry, marginal) and us30fut backtest only; ONE forward leg holds, the proposal's ≥ 3-leg trigger is NOT met, NO PACKAGE EMITS. THE DECOMPOSITION SAYS WHY: late-reach AMPUTATION runs −178 to −689 per cell (25–29 late reachers per forward cell — the journal target has no time limit and most dry-at-3 fires still reach later), against eod savings of 0–84 and stop savings of +66 to +995. THE CAP DOES DODGE THE STOP-CLUSTER CLASS: every stop in every dry set came AFTER bar 3 ("before cap" = 0 on all eight cells), so cap@3 avoids every disaster stop the arm took — but at the amputation price, which exceeds the savings everywhere except where stops were largest (us30fut backtest +995 dodged vs −686 amputated; ger40fut forward +289 vs −322 with +46 eod tipping it). WEEK-32 CHECK: 13 scalp rows survive ex-lockbox in ISO week 32 (the week straddles the excluded span), 6 of them stops, all 6 with MFE ≤ 55 — the referenced class exists in the pre-boundary slice; the cap dodges those six. THE STOP DERIVATION IS UNMEASURABLE at current depth: forward non-reachers number 5–9 per leg (n ≥ 10 required), so no p75 stop prints; the joint proposal waits on accrual on both counts. VERDICT TEXTURE, no decision: a bar-count cap on the scalp is a stop-dodger that pays for itself only when stops are catastrophic; on the real basis it is not a general improvement, and the register-64 ruling's spirit (no time-based harvest) is reinforced from the other side. Accrues weekly; nothing registers.

102. **The circuit-breaker counterfactual — pre-registered and built (operator instinct, 2026-08-28; prereg_circuit_breaker, counted before computation; scalp thread, MEASUREMENT ONLY; backtest/circuit_breaker.py → circuit_breaker.json, embedded in h6_refinement.md weekly).** On the scalp arms' ACTUAL journal rows (plain and refined-entry arms; trades.csv, ex-lockbox, both windows), replayed per instrument × arm in STRICT FILL ORDER within each native trading day (the instrument's session-timezone date): four rules — halt-rest-of-day after k FULL-STOP exits, k ∈ {1, 2}; and, for the same machinery cost, halt after N CONSECUTIVE LOSERS, N ∈ {3, 5}, the milder cousin. The tripping row is taken; the day's remainder is foregone. PER RULE × LEG × ARM × WINDOW: stops dodged (count, points saved); targets and eod outcomes FORFEITED in the halted remainder — the amputation line, priced from the actual foregone rows, targets and eod separately; net vs no-halt = −Σ(foregone points); halt-day count and the dates. BASELINES UNDER IDENTICAL RULES, mandatory: B-RANDOM members simulated under each scalp recipe per instrument (ex-lockbox), the same rules in the same order — member net-delta bands beside every real cell. MOTIVATING ANATOMY cited: the week-32 stop-cluster days (register 101: six stops, all MFE ≤ 55, in the pre-boundary slice). REGISTER 64 DISTINGUISHED: this is EXPOSURE CONTROL, not harvest timing — no time-based exit is introduced, the target is untouched. Survival bar stated in the artifact: a rule survives only if its net vs no-halt clears the member band on ≥ 3 legs; survivors propose as a v1 arm COMPONENT alongside the price-stop — one ratification package, front door; nothing registers from the census. Pinned (tests/test_circuit_breaker.py: strict-order halting, both rule families, the dodged/forfeited decomposition on a synthetic day, twin-run).

**Register 102 first-emission note (2026-08-28 run; quantifiers verified against circuit_breaker.json; plain arm quoted, refined arm in the JSON; statistic shape: per-cell net vs no-halt against the B-RANDOM member band under identical rules).** NO RULE SURVIVES: on every forward leg, every rule's net vs no-halt sits INSIDE its member band (0 of 4 legs clearing for each of the four rules). halt@1stops trips on 4–5 of 8–10 forward days per leg and FORFEITS 14–17 targets per leg against 0–4 dodged stops — net −60.5 (uk100fut), −71.6 (nas100fut), −334.9 (us30fut: sixteen targets forfeited, zero stops dodged) and +68.4 on ger40fut alone (four stops dodged worth +207.6 against +181.0 of targets forfeited) — well inside ger40fut's band max 533.3. halt@2stops rarely trips (0–2 forward halt days; ger40fut +110.4 vs max 375.9, the others −70 / −21 / 0). THE MILDER COUSINS ARE INERT: halt@3losers and halt@5losers trip on ZERO forward days on every leg — an arm that reaches 82% of the time does not string three intraday losers; the five-loser band is identically zero for members too. THE MOTIVATING ANATOMY IS REAL BUT LUCK-PRICED: us30fut BACKTEST is the one place halts pay (+296.9 / +354.8 / +237.3 at k=1 / k=2 / N=3, dodging catastrophic stops worth +620 / +295 / +178), yet random members under the same rules span [−415 .. +1,724] there — the band swallows it. Shape caveat, stated: member bands are wide (±500–1,700 points on the US legs) because random-member scalp books are dominated by a few catastrophic stops — the floor is honest and nearly unclearable at this depth. VERDICT TEXTURE, no decision: on the scalp arms a day-halt after a stop mostly amputates the day's later targets; the exposure-control instinct does not survive its own anatomy on the real rows. Nothing proposes; the section accrues weekly inside the refinement artifact; register 64 stands distinguished (no time-based exit was tested).

(c) FIELD HONESTY: the cell count printed in the header (both ladders: rungs × legs × arms × windows); single-leg glints listed never chained; a candidate needs ≥ 3 of 4 forward legs clearing its member band, same sign; any survivor proposes JOINTLY with (or against) the register-101 derivation as ONE ratification package, front door; nothing registers. **Register 103 first-emission note (2026-08-29 run; quantifiers verified against scalp_risk_grid.json; plain arm quoted, refined arm in the JSON; statistic shapes as pre-registered).** THE BASIS IS SOUND: re-simulating the arms' own fires at the incumbent 2.5×ATR reproduces the journal EXACTLY on every leg and arm (uk100fut 92/92, ger40fut 75/75, nas100fut 67/67, us30fut 68/68 exact matches, max |Δ| 0.0) — the stop ladder prices real fires with the real engine. CANDIDATES: NONE. Two single-leg glints listed, never chained (refined arm 30-min cooldown on ger40fut; plain arm 15-min on nas100fut). FIELD: 96 stop-ladder + 96 cooldown cells stated in the header.
