105. **Per-market specialization doctrine + consistency ruling + the config WATCH page (operator + reviewer, 2026-08-29; prereg_per_market_watch, counted before the committed emission).** (1) THE DOCTRINE: markets are distinct populations with distinct participant psychology; the register SPECIALIZES claims, never defaults. Cross-market replication (≥3-of-4 legs) stays the bar for FIELD screens — it is the luck filter, not an assertion that markets match. Standing practice cited as the doctrine already in force: per-instrument floors, ticks, calendars, spread burdens; per-market questions (Q2-H11) and per-instrument grades (Q3-H6 ×3, register 99). (2) THE PER-MARKET TRACK: a single-market cell may become a question candidate outside the ≥3-leg rule only with ALL of (a) a written MECHANISM SENTENCE — why this market's participants would produce this behavior — operator's pen, falsifiable per cell; (b) a SELECTION-AWARE luck budget: its permutation null computed against the entire in-market config field it was selected from (windows × stops × managements × sessions as applicable) — the best-of-field null, not a single-cell null; (c) its own forward clock at elevated n (≥1.5× the pooled-question minimum; 45 = 1.5×30 PROPOSED at mint, not enacted), graded against forward accrual and the sealed window like every question. (3) CONSISTENCY RULING (operator, verbatim intent: consistency over magnitude): per-market candidates rank and grade on consistency statistics ONLY — median-per-trade, win rate, weekly-consistency count (positive weeks / measurable weeks) — with a TAIL-CARRY CAP: any cell where a single trade exceeds 25% of |net| is tail-carried and non-candidate at any n (register 62's demotion extended); net points print as context only; the selection-aware null is computed on the SAME consistency statistic as the ranking — "most consistent in-market" must beat the most consistent fake the field manufactures; forward clocks grade the same statistics. (4) THE CANDIDATE POOL seeds from existing structure, each with an EMPTY mechanism-sentence slot (nothing advances without it): R-NEXIT-EARLY × open-window (ger40fut + nas100fut, the register-104 recurring glint); us30fut × open-window strength (the ISO-week-32 refutation cell — sitting-side; NOT located in a repo artifact under that name, nearest artifact the event-clock us30fut open cells — flagged); ger40fut S0-H19 reach (register 99's standing WATCH cell); plus future glint-list entries. (5) THE CONFIG WATCH PAGE built: backtest/per_market_watch.py → reports/scoreboard/per_market_watch.md|.json, weekly under Part C after the event clock — per instrument × zone, the best variant / session / slot by consistency statistics, every cell wearing its selection-null percentile, n, and tail-carry check; explicitly a MAP of where specialization would go, never a settings page; cells graduate only by walking (2). Field = management variant × session × event-clock slot (open15 > close15 > news15 > none, register 104's tags reused); the STOP-RUNG AXIS IS NOT RE-SIMULATED (each variant carries its registered stop; the risk grid, register 103, remains the stop ladder's home) — a stated coverage limitation. Implementer-proposed and FLAGGED: a week measurable at ≥3 rows; the slot precedence. (6) FENCES: nothing re-grades, nothing registers from this entry; the track is machinery for future candidates; sealed untouched; shapes per 74-B. FOUNDING TENSION recorded side by side: the operator's framing (different markets, different psychology, different honored hypotheses) and the reviewer's caution (selection-at-scale; streak-luck wears consistency's clothes) — the track exists to hold both. Pinned (tests/test_per_market_watch.py: the tail-carry cap, the weekly count, slot precedence, the best-of-field null's twin-run and direction, the pool's empty slots).

**Register 105 first-emission note (2026-08-29 run; quantifiers verified against per_market_watch.json; shapes: per-cell extremity vs a best-of-field null, field counts).** THE TAIL-CARRY CAP AS WRITTEN IS DEGENERATE FOR NEAR-ZERO-NET CELLS: 293 of 454 measurable cells (8 market×zone fields, 44–63 measurable each) are tail-carried, because a cell netting close to zero makes any one trade a large share of |net| — the cap removes most of the field before ranking, and drains the null too (7–17 of 100 seeds produce no rankable cell on the backtest fields of ger40fut, nas100fut, us30fut; those seeds count as MISSING, never as low values). The rule stands as written; the operator is asked to rule whether the share should be measured against gross (|wins| + |losses|) rather than |net| — a one-constant amendment, front door. WHAT BEATS ITS FIELD'S WIN-RATE NULL (18 non-tail-carried cells across the 8 fields; field null p95 for best win rate 39–50%): mostly small-n and no-slot cells; the scalp arms appear at 100% win on n 12–14 (reach exits under the declared optimistic fill; below the 45-row track floor); TWO forward cells clear both nulls at n ≥ 45 with positive medians and full weekly consistency — nas100fut R-NEXIT-FULL × overlap × no-slot (n=100, win 60%, median +7.3, 3/3 weeks, selection-percentile 100) and us30fut R-ARM15 × dead × no-slot (n=65, win 51%, median +1.24, 3/3 weeks, percentile 99) — LISTED AS FIRST-EMISSION GLINTS for the pool, not added to it: each awaits the operator's mechanism sentence per (4), and neither is a question. THE SEEDED POOL ON THIS PAGE: R-NEXIT-EARLY × open15 sits at the BOTTOM of its fields (ger40fut forward n=17 win 0% median −5.0; nas100fut forward n=11 win 18%, tail-carried) — consistent with register 104's reading of the glint as an AVOIDANCE mechanism (the open window hurts), so its mechanism sentence, if written, is a pause, not an entry; us30fut × open15 forward (n=11–12, win 25–36%, percentile ≤ 48) shows nothing on consistency statistics; the ger40fut S0-H19 reach cell is a signal-level cell and does not appear on a journal-variant page (its home stays bar_persistence_fires.csv). **Register 105 follow-through (operator + reviewer, 2026-08-29; AMENDMENT_per_market_watch_tail_carry_gross, counted; quantifiers verified against the regenerated per_market_watch.json).** (1) TAIL-CARRY CAP AMENDED — logged correction beneath the original, the drafted rule reviewer-attributed: the cap is now share of GROSS PROFIT — a cell where one winning trade exceeds 25% of gross winning points is tail-carried, non-candidate at any n; a cell with no winning trade is tail-carried by convention. 131. **The -REF excursion-window verification — FILL-ANCHORED AND CORRECT; the flag was a timestamp-convention misreading (operator data-integrity flag, 2026-08-31 item 2; verified against clean_finsa/us30fut_1min.csv and the journal row; no Evidence Regression required).** THE WORKED EXAMPLE (the flagged row, reconstructed exactly): us30fut 2026-08-04 open_ts 13:56 R-SCALP-REF short, fill 53,862.6, MFE 55.5, MAE 333.5, time-to-MFE 1 min. THE CONVENTION: the simulate() time axis is CLOSE-STAMPED — Env.trail_ref derives a bar's open as ts − 1M — so the journal's open_ts/close_ts are BAR-CLOSE stamps and THE FILL IS THE OPEN OF THE BAR THAT CLOSES AT open_ts: the fill bar OPENS 13:55 at exactly 53,862.6 (touchable trivially — it IS that bar's open); MFE 55.5 = fill − the next bar's low (53,807.1, the bar closing 13:57 → 1 min ✓); MAE 333.5 = the hold's max high (54,196.1) − fill ✓ — every recorded number reproduces to the decimal under the close-stamp reading. THE FLAG'S ANATOMY: reading open_ts against the store's OPEN-STAMPED bars mislocates the fill by one minute — the 13:56–13:59 open-stamped highs (max 53,866.1) indeed never reach 53,862.6+, which is what made the fill look untouchable. THE CONVENTION IS JOURNAL-WIDE, not -REF-specific (every variant shares the axis; the -REF pipeline is additionally sound: refine() returns the trigger bar and simulate enters the NEXT bar's open — no lookahead); enrichment MAE/MFE join the same close-stamped axis and are FILL-ANCHORED — correct as built. BLAST RADIUS: NONE (no defect; no consumer misreads internally — the axis is shared end-to-end); the one external consumer of the raw timestamps is the HUMAN reading them against the store, so the convention is now DOCUMENTED where humans read: VARIANTS.md header (generated, with the anatomy), the glossary (open_ts/close_ts entry), and this entry as the worked example; PINNED (tests/test_h1_harvest.py::test_timestamp_convention_close_stamped_fill_anchor). SIDE-NOTE ON REGISTER 130's forensic: its open-clock labels (13:45–14:47) shift one minute earlier under the correct reading — immaterial to the volatility finding, stated.
